# Project: Voxel Engine
Simple voxel engine with cubic chunks and infinite terrain using value-noise function.
# Start of VoxelThing.sb3

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grid" - a 16x16 png containing a 2x2 white/grey checkerboard pattern
2. "box" - a 16x16 png of stereotypical box, with border around the edge and X-shape in the middle
3. "uv grid" - a 256x256 png of horizontal left-to-right 0-to-255 red gradient, vertical top-to-bottom 0-to-255 green gradient, and a 4x4 grid on top of the gradient. Top left corner has label U:0 V:0, top right corner has label U:1 V:0, bottom left corner has label U:0 V:1, bottom right corner has label U:1 V:1.
4. "stoneByVadik1,NOTfromMinecraft" - a 16x16 cobblestone texture made by Vadik1 to serve as a test texture

```scratchblocks
define Generate mesh for chunk (chunk name) (chunk id)
delete all of [tmpChunkX v]
delete all of [tmpChunkY v]
delete all of [tmpChunkZ v]
delete all of [tmpChunkU v]
delete all of [tmpChunkV v]
delete all of [tmpChunkBrightness v]
set [sz v] to [1]
repeat (16)
  set [sy v] to [1]
  repeat (16)
    set [sx v] to [1]
    repeat (16)
      if <(get tmp block at (sx) (sy) (sz) :: custom) > [0]> then
        add face [1] [2] [3] [4] <(get tmp block at (sx) (sy) ((sz) - (1)) :: custom) = [0]> :: custom
        add face [5] [6] [7] [8] <(get tmp block at (sx) (sy) ((sz) + (1)) :: custom) = [0]> :: custom
        add face [9] [10] [11] [12] <(get tmp block at (sx) ((sy) - (1)) (sz) :: custom) = [0]> :: custom
        add face [13] [14] [15] [16] <(get tmp block at (sx) ((sy) + (1)) (sz) :: custom) = [0]> :: custom
        add face [17] [18] [19] [20] <(get tmp block at ((sx) - (1)) (sy) (sz) :: custom) = [0]> :: custom
        add face [21] [22] [23] [24] <(get tmp block at ((sx) + (1)) (sy) (sz) :: custom) = [0]> :: custom
      end
      change [sx v] by (1)
    end
    change [sy v] by (1)
  end
  change [sz v] by (1)
end
if <(length of [tmpChunkX v]) > [0]> then
  replace item (chunk id) of [chunkHasMesh v] with [2]
  Simple3D | create mesh (chunk name)
  Simple3D | set (chunk name) positions XYZ [tmpChunkX v] [tmpChunkY v] [tmpChunkZ v]
  Simple3D | set (chunk name) texture coordinates UV [tmpChunkU v] [tmpChunkV v]
  Simple3D | set (chunk name) colors RGB [tmpChunkBrightness v] [tmpChunkBrightness v] [tmpChunkBrightness v]
  Simple3D | make (chunk name) inherit from meshes [chunkBase]
else
  replace item (chunk id) of [chunkHasMesh v] with [1]
end

define get noise at (x) (y) (z)
set [ix v] to ([floor v] of (x))
set [iy v] to ([floor v] of (y))
set [iz v] to ([floor v] of (z))
set [fx v] to ((x) - (ix))
set [fy v] to ((y) - (iy))
set [fz v] to ((z) - (iz))
return (mix (mix (mix (rng+ [0] [0] [0] :: custom) (rng+ [1] [0] [0] :: custom) (fx) :: custom) (mix (rng+ [0] [1] [0] :: custom) (rng+ [1] [1] [0] :: custom) (fx) :: custom) (fy) :: custom) (mix (mix (rng+ [0] [0] [1] :: custom) (rng+ [1] [0] [1] :: custom) (fx) :: custom) (mix (rng+ [0] [1] [1] :: custom) (rng+ [1] [1] [1] :: custom) (fx) :: custom) (fy) :: custom) (fz) :: custom) :: custom

define mix (a) (b) (t)
return ((a) + (((b) - (a)) * (t))) :: custom

define rng+ (x) (y) (z)
return (random ((ix) + (x)) ((iy) + (y)) ((iz) + (z)) :: custom) :: custom

define random (x) (y) (z)
return (((((x) * (((z) * (x)) - (0.1))) * (([cos v] of ((((y) * (3.87)) + (x)) * (57.29577951308232))) * (365.3))) + (((((z) - (y)) * (578.9)) / (([sin v] of (((z) / ((1.243) + (x))) * (57.29577951308232))) + (0.1))) + ((z) / (((y) + (x)) + (0.5))))) mod (1)) :: custom

define Generate chunk (cx) (cy) (cz)
set [my chunk id v] to ((length of [chunkNames v]) + (1))
set [chunk offset v] to ((length of [chunkBlocks v]) + (1))
add (cx) to [chunkX v]
add (cy) to [chunkY v]
add (cz) to [chunkZ v]
add [0] to [chunkUpdates v]
add [0] to [chunkHasMesh v]
add (chunk name (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom) to [chunkNames v]
repeat ((16) * ((16) * (16)))
  add [0] to [chunkBlocks v]
end
set [neighbors found v] to [0]
set [sz v] to [0]
repeat (3)
  set [sy v] to [0]
  repeat (3)
    set [sx v] to [0]
    repeat (3)
      set [other chunk id v] to (get chunk ((cx) + ((sx) - (1))) ((cy) + ((sy) - (1))) ((cz) + ((sz) - (1))) :: custom)
      if <(other chunk id) > [0]> then
        replace item ((((other chunk id) - (1)) * (27)) + (((27) - (sx)) - (((sy) * (3)) + ((sz) * (9))))) of [chunkNeighbors v] with (my chunk id)
        replace item (other chunk id) of [chunkNeightborCount v] with ((item (other chunk id) of [chunkNeightborCount v]) + (1))
        if <(item (other chunk id) of [chunkNeightborCount v]) = [27]> then
          add (other chunk id) to [chunks that need a mesh v]
        end
        change [neighbors found v] by (1)
      end
      add (other chunk id) to [chunkNeighbors v]
      change [sx v] by (1)
    end
    change [sy v] by (1)
  end
  change [sz v] by (1)
end
add (neighbors found) to [chunkNeightborCount v]
if <(neighbors found) = [27]> then
  add (my chunk id) to [chunks that need a mesh v]
end
set [sz v] to [0]
repeat (16)
  set [sy v] to [0]
  repeat (16)
    set [sx v] to [0]
    repeat (16)
      set [block v] to [1]
      set [noise v] to ((((sy) + ((cy) * (16))) - (128)) / (32))
      change [noise v] by (get noise at (((sx) / (8)) + ((cx) * (2))) (((sy) / (8)) + ((cy) * (2))) (((sz) / (8)) + ((cz) * (2))) :: custom)
      change [noise v] by ((2) * (get noise at (((sx) / (32)) + ((cx) / (2))) (((sy) / (32)) + ((cy) / (2))) (((sz) / (32)) + ((cz) / (2))) :: custom))
      if <(noise) > [0.5]> then
        set [block v] to [0]
      end
      set chunk block (block) at (sx) (sy) (sz) :: custom
      change [sx v] by (1)
    end
    change [sy v] by (1)
  end
  change [sz v] by (1)
end

define Copy section (x1) (y1) (z1) to (x2) (y2) (z2) offset (xt) (yt) (zt)

  set [sz v] to (z1)
  repeat (((z2) - (z1)) + (1))
    set [sy v] to (y1)

set [chunk offset v] to ((((item # of (chunk name (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom) in [chunkNames v]) - (1)) * (4096)) + (1))

define chunk name (cx) (cy) (cz)
return (join (join (cx) [/]) (join (cy) (join [/] (cz)))) :: custom

define get chunk (cx) (cy) (cz)
return (item # of (chunk name (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom) in [chunkNames v]) :: custom

define Draw
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
set [index v] to [0]
repeat (length of [drawable chunks v])
  change [index v] by (1)
  set [my chunk id v] to (item (index) of [drawable chunks v])
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | move X:((item (my chunk id) of [chunkX v]) * (16)) Y:((item (my chunk id) of [chunkY v]) * (16)) Z:((item (my chunk id) of [chunkZ v]) * (16))
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:((0.5) + ((0.5) * <((item (my chunk id) of [chunkX v]) mod (2)) = [0]>)) G:((0.5) + ((0.5) * <((item (my chunk id) of [chunkY v]) mod (2)) = [0]>)) B:((0.5) + ((0.5) * <((item (my chunk id) of [chunkZ v]) mod (2)) = [0]>)) A:(1)
  Simple3D | draw mesh (item (my chunk id) of [chunkNames v])
  if <(DEBUG: chunk borders visible) = [1]> then
    Simple3D | scale X:(16) Y:(16) Z:(16)
    Simple3D | draw mesh [outline]
  end
end

define Load chunks
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
delete all of [drawable chunks v]
set [can generate v] to [1]
if <key (l v) pressed?> then
  set [can generate v] to [20]
end
set [cx v] to ([floor v] of ((camX) / (16)))
set [cy v] to ([floor v] of ((camY) / (16)))
set [cz v] to ([floor v] of ((camZ) / (16)))
set [my chunk id v] to (get chunk (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom)
if <(my chunk id) = [0]> then
  Generate chunk (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom
else
  delete all of [traverseId v]
  add (my chunk id) to [traverseId v]
  repeat until <(length of [traverseId v]) = [0]>
    set [my chunk id v] to (item (1) of [traverseId v])
    delete (1) of [traverseId v]
    set [cx v] to (item (my chunk id) of [chunkX v])
    set [cy v] to (item (my chunk id) of [chunkY v])
    set [cz v] to (item (my chunk id) of [chunkZ v])
    if <(item (my chunk id) of [chunkUpdates v]) < (timer)> then
      replace item (my chunk id) of [chunkUpdates v] with (timer)
      Simple3D | transform X:(((cx) * (16)) + (8)) Y:(((cy) * (16)) + (8)) Z:(((cz) * (16)) + (8))
      if <can i see this? (Simple3D | transformed [X v]) (Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) (Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) :: custom> then
        if <(item (my chunk id) of [chunkHasMesh v]) = [2]> then
          add (my chunk id) to [drawable chunks v]
        end
        set [sx v] to ((((cx) * (16)) + (8)) - (camX))
        set [sy v] to ((((cy) * (16)) + (8)) - (camY))
        set [sz v] to ((((cz) * (16)) + (8)) - (camZ))
        if <(((sx) * (sx)) + (((sy) * (sy)) + ((sz) * (sz)))) < ((render distance) * (render distance))> then
          set [index v] to (((my chunk id) - (1)) * (27))
          set [index 2 v] to (((my chunk id) - (1)) * (6))
          traverse chunk2 (item ((index) + (13)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) (cy) (cz) (item ((index 2) + (1)) of [chunkSideSolidBlockCount v]) :: custom
          traverse chunk2 (item ((index) + (15)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) (cy) (cz) (item ((index 2) + (2)) of [chunkSideSolidBlockCount v]) :: custom
          traverse chunk2 (item ((index) + (11)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) ((cy) - (1)) (cz) (item ((index 2) + (3)) of [chunkSideSolidBlockCount v]) :: custom
          traverse chunk2 (item ((index) + (17)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) ((cy) + (1)) (cz) (item ((index 2) + (4)) of [chunkSideSolidBlockCount v]) :: custom
          traverse chunk2 (item ((index) + (5)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) (cy) ((cz) - (1)) (item ((index 2) + (5)) of [chunkSideSolidBlockCount v]) :: custom
          traverse chunk2 (item ((index) + (23)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) (cy) ((cz) + (1)) (item ((index 2) + (6)) of [chunkSideSolidBlockCount v]) :: custom
          if <<(item (my chunk id) of [chunkHasMesh v]) = [0]> and <(item (my chunk id) of [chunkSolidBlockCount v]) > [0]>> then
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (1)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) ((cy) - (1)) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (2)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) ((cy) - (1)) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (3)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) ((cy) - (1)) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (4)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) (cy) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (6)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) (cy) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (7)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) ((cy) + (1)) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (8)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) ((cy) + (1)) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (9)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) ((cy) + (1)) ((cz) - (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (10)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) ((cy) - (1)) (cz) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (12)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) ((cy) - (1)) (cz) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (16)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) ((cy) + (1)) (cz) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (18)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) ((cy) + (1)) (cz) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (19)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) ((cy) - (1)) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (20)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) ((cy) - (1)) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (21)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) ((cy) - (1)) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (22)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) (cy) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (24)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) (cy) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (25)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) - (1)) ((cy) + (1)) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (26)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) (cx) ((cy) + (1)) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
            traverse chunk3 (item ((index) + (27)) of [chunkNeighbors v]) ((cx) + (1)) ((cy) + (1)) ((cz) + (1)) :: custom
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end
end
repeat until <(length of [chunks that need a mesh v]) = [0]>
  set [my chunk id v] to (item (1) of [chunks that need a mesh v])
  if <(item (my chunk id) of [chunkSolidBlockCount v]) > [0]> then
    Copy all chunk data for (item (my chunk id) of [chunkX v]) (item (my chunk id) of [chunkY v]) (item (my chunk id) of [chunkZ v]) (my chunk id) :: custom
  end
  delete (1) of [chunks that need a mesh v]
end

set [cz v] to [-1]
repeat (4)
  set [cy v] to [-1]
  repeat (4)
    set [cx v] to [-1]
    repeat (4)
      Generate chunk (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom
      change [cx v] by (1)
    end
    change [cy v] by (1)
  end
  change [cz v] by (1)
end

define traverse chunk2 (id) (cx) (cy) (cz) (side block count)
if <(id) = [0]> then
  if <(can generate) > [0]> then
    Generate chunk (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom
    change [can generate v] by (-1)
  end
else
  if <(side block count) < [256]> then
    add (id) to [traverseId v]
  end
end

define can i see this? (x) (y) (z)
if <(z) > (bounding radius 3D)> then
  return <[1] = [2]> :: custom
else
  return <<((([abs v] of (x)) * (visCoefX-X)) - ((z) * (visCoefX-Z))) < (bounding radius 3D)> and <((([abs v] of (y)) * (visCoefY-Y)) - ((z) * (visCoefY-Z))) < (bounding radius 3D)>> :: custom
end

when flag clicked
set [render distance v] to ((6) * (16))
set [bounding radius 3D v] to ([sqrt v] of (((8) * (8)) * (3)))
set [bounding radius squared 3D v] to (((8) * (8)) * (3))
set [FOV v] to [90]
set [viewFactor v] to (([tan v] of ((90) - ((FOV) / (2)))) * (Simple3D | render target [aspect ratio v]))
set [distance v] to ([sqrt v] of ((1) + ((viewFactor) * (viewFactor))))
set [visCoefX-Z v] to (((0) - (viewFactor)) / (distance))
set [visCoefX-X v] to ((1) / (distance))
set [viewFactor v] to ([tan v] of ((90) - ((FOV) / (2))))
set [distance v] to ([sqrt v] of ((1) + ((viewFactor) * (viewFactor))))
set [visCoefY-Z v] to (((0) - (viewFactor)) / (distance))
set [visCoefY-Y v] to ((1) / (distance))

define count section (x1) (y1) (z1) to (x2) (y2) (z2)
set [solid block count v] to [0]
if <(chunk offset) > [0]> then
  set [sz v] to (z1)
  repeat (((z2) - (z1)) + (1))
    set [sy v] to (y1)
    repeat (((y2) - (y1)) + (1))
      set [sx v] to (x1)
      repeat (((x2) - (x1)) + (1))
        if <(get chunk block at (sx) (sy) (sz) :: custom) > [0]> then
          change [solid block count v] by (1)
        end
        change [sx v] by (1)
      end
      change [sy v] by (1)
    end
    change [sz v] by (1)
  end
end

define traverse chunk3 (id) (cx) (cy) (cz)
if <(id) = [0]> then
  if <(can generate) > [0]> then
    Generate chunk (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom
    change [can generate v] by (-1)
  end
end

define get world block at (x) (y) (z)
select chunk ([floor v] of ((x) / (16))) ([floor v] of ((y) / (16))) ([floor v] of ((z) / (16))) :: custom
if <(chunk offset) < [1]> then
  return [0] :: custom
else
  return (get chunk block at ((x) mod (16)) ((y) mod (16)) ((z) mod (16)) :: custom) :: custom
end

define player collides
set [detections v] to [0]
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) - (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (1.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) - (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) + (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (1.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) - (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) - (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (1.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) + (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) + (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (1.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) + (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) - (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (0.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) - (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) + (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (0.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) - (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) - (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (0.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) + (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) + (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) - (0.5))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) + (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) - (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) + (0.2))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) - (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) + (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) + (0.2))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) - (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) - (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) + (0.2))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) + (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
change [detections v] by <(cached get world block at ([floor v] of ((camX) + (0.3))) ([floor v] of ((camY) + (0.2))) ([floor v] of ((camZ) + (0.3))) :: custom) > [0]>
return <(detections) > [0]> :: custom

define cached get world block at (x) (y) (z)
if <not <<([floor v] of ((x) / (16))) = (cx)> and <<([floor v] of ((y) / (16))) = (cy)> and <([floor v] of ((z) / (16))) = (cz)>>>> then
  set [cx v] to ([floor v] of ((x) / (16)))
  set [cy v] to ([floor v] of ((y) / (16)))
  set [cz v] to ([floor v] of ((z) / (16)))
  select chunk (cx) (cy) (cz) :: custom
end
if <(chunk offset) < [1]> then
  return [0] :: custom
else
  return (get chunk block at ((x) mod (16)) ((y) mod (16)) ((z) mod (16)) :: custom) :: custom
end
```

```scratchblocks
  Simple3D | start with no transformation

when flag clicked
set [sound v] to [rain_thunder_loop]
create clone of (_myself_ v)
set [sound v] to [rain_close]
create clone of (_myself_ v)
set [sound v] to [wind2]
create clone of (_myself_ v)

define Create sky
Simple3D | create mesh [sky]
Simple3D | set [sky] indices [skyIdx v]
Simple3D | set [sky] positions XYZ [skyX v] [skyY v] [skyZ v]
Simple3D | set [sky] texture coordinates UVW [skyX v] [skyY v] [skyZ v]
Simple3D | set [sky] cube texture (X+ v) (Simple3D | texture from costume (graycloud_ft v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [sky] cube texture (X- v) (Simple3D | texture from costume (graycloud_bk v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [sky] cube texture (Y+ v) (Simple3D | texture from costume (graycloud_up v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [sky] cube texture (Y- v) (Simple3D | texture from costume (graycloud_dn v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [sky] cube texture (Z+ v) (Simple3D | texture from costume (graycloud_rt v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [sky] cube texture (Z- v) (Simple3D | texture from costume (graycloud_lf v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000)

define Create rain
delete all of [rainPos v]
repeat (10000)
  add (pick random (-100.0) to (100.0)) to [rainPos v]
  add (pick random (0) to (100.0)) to [rainPos v]
  add (pick random (-100.0) to (100.0)) to [rainPos v]
end
Simple3D | create mesh [rain]
Simple3D | set [rain] primitives (lines v)
Simple3D | set [rain] positions XYZ [rainX v] [rainY v] [rainZ v]
Simple3D | [rain] optimize next uploaded list for being (frequently fully v) updated
Simple3D | set [rain] instance [XYZ positions v] [rainPos v]

define tick rain
set [i v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [rainPos v]) / (3))
  change [i v] by (3)
  replace item ((i) - (1)) of [rainPos v] with ((item ((i) - (1)) of [rainPos v]) - (4.7))
  if <(item ((i) - (1)) of [rainPos v]) < [0]> then
    replace item ((i) - (1)) of [rainPos v] with (pick random (100.0) to (110.0))
  end
  replace item ((i) - (2)) of [rainPos v] with ((((item ((i) - (2)) of [rainPos v]) - ((camX) - (100))) mod (200)) + ((camX) - (100)))
  replace item ((i) - (0)) of [rainPos v] with ((((item ((i) - (0)) of [rainPos v]) - ((camZ) - (100))) mod (200)) + ((camZ) - (100)))
end
Simple3D | set [rain] instance [XYZ positions v] [rainPos v]

define Create grass
if <(mode) < [3]> then
  if <(mode) < [2]> then
    Make grass [10000] [25.0] :: custom
  else
    Make grass [100000] [50.0] :: custom
  end
else
  if <(mode) < [4]> then
    Make grass [400000] [100.0] :: custom
  else
    Make grass [1000000] [100.0] :: custom
  end
end

when I start as a clone
if <(sound) = [rain_close]> then
  forever
    set volume to ((100) / (camY))%
  end
end
if <(sound) = [wind2]> then
  forever
    set volume to ((10) * ([sqrt v] of (((camVelX) * (camVelX)) + (((camVelY) * (camVelY)) + ((camVelZ) * (camVelZ))))))%
  end
end

when I start as a clone
forever
  play sound (sound) until done
end
```

# Project: 100k Particles
100k instanced particles, with colors and 8 frames of animation. Physics and animation are done by the project and the resulting lists are streamed into the extension.
# Start of InstancedParticlesUV.sb3
## Sprite: "Stage"

### Costumes
1. "backdrop1" - empty svg

### Global Variables
camX = 157.8813892699441
camY = -3.846760309207592
camZ = 25.72629732261853
camRotX = 180
camRotY = 216
i = 400000
j = 7
step = 2749
fps = 24.186046511627907
frames = 9

### Global Lists
x = [-1,1,-1,-1,1,1]
y = [-1,-1,1,1,-1,1]
u = [0,1,0,0,1,1]
v = [0,0,1,1,0,1]
instances = []
vel = []
instUV = []
instRGBA = []

### Blocks

```scratchblocks
// Configuration for https://turbowarp.org/\nYou can move, resize, and minimize this comment, but don't edit it by hand. This comment can be deleted to remove the stored settings.\n{"framerate":60,"hq":true,"width":640} // _twconfig_
```

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "star" - white 4-sided star shape
2. "effect1" - texture atlas of 2x2 frames of animation of swirl spinning and shrinking
3. "effect2" - texture atlas of 4x2 frames of animation of swirl spinning and shrinking

### Blocks
```scratchblocks
when flag clicked
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
Simple3D | create mesh [particle]
Simple3D | set [particle] positions XY [x v] [y v]
Simple3D | set [particle] texture coordinates UV [u v] [v v]
Simple3D | set [particle] billboarding (true v)
Simple3D | set [particle] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (effect2 v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [particle] discord pixels less opaque than [0.5], for those that pass true
Simple3D | [particle] optimize next uploaded list for being (frequently fully v) updated
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000)
create particles :: custom
set [step v] to [0]
forever
  controls :: custom
  tick particles :: custom
  draw :: custom
  measure fps :: custom
end

define controls
set [camRotX v] to (mouse y)
set [camRotY v] to ((0) - (mouse x))
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (() - (camRotX)) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (() - (camRotY)) degrees
Simple3D | transform direction X:(<key (d v) pressed?> - <key (a v) pressed?>) Y:(0) Z:(<key (s v) pressed?> - <key (w v) pressed?>) from [view space v] to [world space v]
change [camX v] by (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
change [camY v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
change [camZ v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
Simple3D | move X:(() - (camX)) Y:(() - (camY)) Z:(() - (camZ))

define draw
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Simple3D | draw mesh [particle]

define tick particles
set [i v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [instances v]) / (3))
  replace item ((i) + (2)) of [vel v] with ((item ((i) + (2)) of [vel v]) - (0.01))
  replace item ((i) + (1)) of [instances v] with ((item ((i) + (1)) of [instances v]) + (item ((i) + (1)) of [vel v]))
  replace item ((i) + (2)) of [instances v] with ((item ((i) + (2)) of [instances v]) + (item ((i) + (2)) of [vel v]))
  replace item ((i) + (3)) of [instances v] with ((item ((i) + (3)) of [instances v]) + (item ((i) + (3)) of [vel v]))
  if <(item ((i) + (2)) of [instances v]) < [-20]> then
    replace item ((i) + (1)) of [vel v] with ((item ((i) + (1)) of [vel v]) * (0.9))
    replace item ((i) + (2)) of [vel v] with ((item ((i) + (2)) of [vel v]) * (-0.6))
    replace item ((i) + (3)) of [vel v] with ((item ((i) + (3)) of [vel v]) * (0.9))
    if <(([abs v] of (item ((i) + (1)) of [vel v])) + (([abs v] of (item ((i) + (2)) of [vel v])) + ([abs v] of (item ((i) + (3)) of [vel v])))) < [1]> then
      replace item ((i) + (1)) of [vel v] with ((pick random (-0.1) to (0.1)) + (1))
      replace item ((i) + (2)) of [vel v] with ((pick random (-0.1) to (0.1)) + (0.5))
      replace item ((i) + (3)) of [vel v] with (pick random (-0.5) to (0.5))
      replace item ((i) + (1)) of [instances v] with (pick random (-0.3) to (0.3))
      replace item ((i) + (2)) of [instances v] with ((pick random (-0.3) to (0.3)) + (5))
      replace item ((i) + (3)) of [instances v] with (pick random (-0.3) to (0.3))
    end
  end
  change [i v] by (3)
end
Simple3D | set [particle] instance [XYZ positions v] [instances v]
set [i v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [instUV v]) / (4))
  if <((((i) / (4)) + (step)) mod (6)) = [0]> then
    set [j v] to (((item ((i) + (1)) of [instUV v]) * (4)) + (((item ((i) + (2)) of [instUV v]) * (8)) + (1)))
    replace item ((i) + (1)) of [instUV v] with ((0.25) * ((j) mod (4)))
    replace item ((i) + (2)) of [instUV v] with ((0.5) * (([floor v] of ((j) / (4))) mod (2)))
  end
  change [i v] by (4)
end
Simple3D | set [particle] instance [UV offsets and sizes v] [instUV v]
change [step v] by (1)

define create particles
delete all of [instances v]
delete all of [instUV v]
delete all of [instRGBA v]
delete all of [vel v]
repeat (100000)
  repeat (3)
    add (pick random (-20.0) to (20.0)) to [instances v]
    add (pick random (-0.05) to (0.05)) to [vel v]
    add (pick random (0.1) to (1)) to [instRGBA v]
  end
  add ((pick random (0) to (3)) * (0.25)) to [instUV v]
  add ((pick random (0) to (1)) * (0.5)) to [instUV v]
  add [0.25] to [instUV v]
  add [0.5] to [instUV v]
end
Simple3D | set [particle] instance [XYZ positions v] [instances v]
Simple3D | set [particle] instance [UV offsets and sizes v] [instUV v]
Simple3D | set [particle] instance [RGB colors v] [instRGBA v]

define measure fps
change [frames v] by (1)
if <(timer) > [1]> then
  set [fps v] to ((frames) / (timer))
  set [frames v] to [0]
  reset timer
end

```


# End of InstancedParticlesUV.sb3

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grass" - bright grass texture
2. "2x2" - 2x2 texture atlas of stone, cobblestone, dirt and grass textures
3. "cyan glow" - cyan glow
4. "yellow glow" - yellow glow

### Blocks
```scratchblocks
when flag clicked
pointerlock | set pointer lock (true v)
Setup fog :: custom
Create plane :: custom
Create cone :: custom
Create boxes :: custom
Create glow :: custom
Create fullscreen :: custom
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000)
Create lights [5] :: custom
forever
  controls :: custom
  draw :: custom
end

define controls
change [camRotX v] by ((mouse y) * (0.5))
change [camRotY v] by ((mouse x) * (-0.5))
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (() - (camRotX)) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (() - (camRotY)) degrees
change [camY v] by ((<key (space v) pressed?> - <key (join [shift] []) pressed?>) * (0.5))
Simple3D | transform direction X:((<key (d v) pressed?> - <key (a v) pressed?>) * (0.5)) Y:(0) Z:((<key (s v) pressed?> - <key (w v) pressed?>) * (0.5)) from [view space v] to [world space v]
change [camX v] by (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
change [camY v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
change [camZ v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
Simple3D | move X:(() - (camX)) Y:(() - (camY)) Z:(() - (camZ))

define draw
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
Draw plane :: custom
Draw box :: custom
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (false v)
Draw lights :: custom
Draw glow :: custom

Simple3D | when canvas resolution changes
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000)

define gen cone (steps) (radius) (length)
delete all of [coneX v]
delete all of [coneY v]
delete all of [coneZ v]
Simple3D | configure [custom v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
add [0] to [coneX v]
add [0] to [coneY v]
add [0] to [coneZ v]
repeat ((steps) + (1))
  Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((360) / (steps)) degrees
  Simple3D | transform X:(radius) Y:((0) - (length)) Z:(0)
  add (Simple3D | transformed [X v]) to [coneX v]
  add (Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) to [coneY v]
  add (Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) to [coneZ v]
end

define Create plane
Simple3D | create mesh [plane]
Simple3D | set [plane] positions XYZ [planeX v] [planeY v] [planeZ v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture coordinates UV [planeU v] [planeV v]
Simple3D | set [plane] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [plane] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (grass v)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture mipmapping [smooth transitions v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture anisotropic filtering (16 v)
Simple3D | set [plane] blending (overwrite color (fastest for opaque) v)

define Create cone
gen cone [50] [20] [50] :: custom
Simple3D | create mesh [cone]
Simple3D | set [cone] positions XYZ [coneX v] [coneY v] [coneZ v]
Simple3D | set [cone] primitives (triangle fan v)
Simple3D | create mesh [coneAdd]
Simple3D | make [coneAdd] inherit from meshes [cone]
Simple3D | set [coneAdd] blending (additive v)
Simple3D | set [coneAdd] cull (back faces v)
Simple3D | create mesh [coneSub]
Simple3D | make [coneSub] inherit from meshes [cone]
Simple3D | set [coneSub] blending (subtractive v)
Simple3D | set [coneSub] cull (front faces v)

define Create boxes
Simple3D | create mesh [box]
Simple3D | set [box] indices [boxIdx v]
Simple3D | set [box] positions XYZ [boxX v] [boxY v] [boxZ v]
Simple3D | set [box] texture coordinates UV [boxU v] [boxV v]
Simple3D | set [box] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [box] cull (back faces v)
Simple3D | set [box] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (2x2 v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [box] blending (overwrite color (fastest for opaque) v)
Simple3D | set [box] accurate interpolation (true v)
delete all of [boxTr v]
delete all of [boxUV v]
repeat (1000)
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | move X:(pick random (-100) to (100)) Y:(pick random (2) to (6)) Z:(pick random (-100) to (100))
  Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (pick random (-180) to (180)) degrees
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (pick random (-180) to (180)) degrees
  Simple3D | save into [boxTr v] at ((length of [boxTr v]) + (1))
  add ((pick random (0) to (1)) * (0.5)) to [boxUV v]
  add ((pick random (0) to (1)) * (0.5)) to [boxUV v]
  add [0.5] to [boxUV v]
  add [0.5] to [boxUV v]
end
Simple3D | set [box] instance [transforms v] [boxTr v]
Simple3D | set [box] instance [UV offsets and sizes v] [boxUV v]

define Draw plane
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1) G:(1) B:(1) A:(1)
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | scale X:(200) Y:(100) Z:(200)
Simple3D | draw mesh [plane]

define Draw box
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.9) G:(0.9) B:(0.9) A:(1)
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | draw mesh [box]

define Create lights (amount)
delete all of [lights v]
repeat ((amount) * (7))
  add (pick random (-100.0) to (100.0)) to [lights v]
end

define Setup fog
Simple3D | turn fog (true v)
Simple3D | set fog distance near:(141) far:(145)
Simple3D | set fog color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0.1)
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0.1) A:(1)
Simple3D | set fog world space origin at X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(0)

define Create glow
Simple3D | create mesh [glow]
Simple3D | set [glow] positions XY [planeX v] [planeZ v]
Simple3D | set [glow] texture coordinates UV [glowU v] [glowV v]
Simple3D | set [glow] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [glow] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (yellow glow v)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [glow] blending (additive v)
Simple3D | set [glow] billboarding (true v)

define Draw glow
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1) G:(1) B:(1) A:(1)
set [light v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [lights v]) / (7))
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | move X:(item ((light) + (1)) of [lights v]) Y:(0) Z:(item ((light) + (2)) of [lights v])
  Simple3D | move X:((7) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (3)) of [lights v])) * (23)))) Y:((20) + ((5) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (4)) of [lights v])) * (30))))) Z:((3) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (5)) of [lights v])) * (37))))
  Simple3D | scale X:(2) Y:(2) Z:(2)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [glow]
  change [light v] by (7)
end

define Draw lights
set [light v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [lights v]) / (7))
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | move X:(item ((light) + (1)) of [lights v]) Y:(0) Z:(item ((light) + (2)) of [lights v])
  Simple3D | move X:((7) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (3)) of [lights v])) * (23)))) Y:((20) + ((5) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (4)) of [lights v])) * (30))))) Z:((3) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (5)) of [lights v])) * (37))))
  Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (((30) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (6)) of [lights v])) * (41)))) + ((30) * ([cos v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (7)) of [lights v])) * (27))))) degrees
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (30) degrees
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.5) G:(0.4) B:(0.1) A:(0.5)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [coneAdd]
  Simple3D | transform X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(0) from [view space v] to [model space v]
  if <<(Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) < [0]> and <([sqrt v] of (((Simple3D | transformed [X v]) * (Simple3D | transformed [X v])) + ((Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) * (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])))) < (((Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) / (-50)) * (20))>> then
    Simple3D | wrapper {
      Simple3D | depth test (everything v) write (false v)
      Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
      Simple3D | start with no transformation
      Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
      Simple3D | start with no transformation
      Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
      Simple3D | start with no transformation
      Simple3D | draw mesh [fullscreen]
      Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (false v)
    }
  end
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.5) G:(0.4) B:(0.1) A:(0.45)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [coneSub]
  change [light v] by (7)
end

Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.1) G:(0.4) B:(0.5) A:(0.5)
Simple3D | draw mesh [coneAdd]
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.1) G:(0.4) B:(0.5) A:(0.45)
Simple3D | draw mesh [coneSub]
change [light v] by (7)

define Create fullscreen
Simple3D | create mesh [fullscreen]
Simple3D | set [fullscreen] positions XY [planeX v] [planeZ v]
Simple3D | set [fullscreen] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [fullscreen] blending (additive v)

// Textures are drawn by Vadik1/Xeltalliv and licensed under CC BY-SA 4.0.\n(CC BY-SA 4.0 is not compatible with scratch website)\nThis cobblestone texture have been posted (and became widespread, with most people either not crediting me, or falsely crediting minecraft) on scratch, so it's CC BY-SA 2.0.\n\nDo whatever you want with the code.
```

# Project: Window rendering

# Start of LightingWindowed.sb3
## Sprite: "Stage"

### Costumes
1. "backdrop1" - empty svg

### Global Variables
camX = -70
camY = 2
camZ = 78
camRotX = -9
camRotY = 138
light = 35
windowCount = 3
i = 1
mouse X dir = ""
mouse Y dir = ""
cursor = "default"
windowI = 0
j = 0
mode = "none"
last mouse X = -247
last mouse Y = 180
dx = 0
dy = 0
pointer lock = 0

### Global Lists
planeX = [-1,1,-1,1]
planeY = [0,0,0,0]
planeU = [0,200,0,200]
planeV = [0,0,200,200]
coneX = []
coneY = []

```scratchblocks
when flag clicked

  Change cursor :: custom

define controls

  if <pointerlock | pointer locked?> then
    change [camRotX v] by ((mouse y) * (0.5))
    change [camRotY v] by ((mouse x) * (-0.5))
  end
end
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation

if <<(i) = (windowI)> and <(mode) = [focus]>> then
  change [camY v] by ((<key (space v) pressed?> - <key (join [shift] []) pressed?>) * (0.5))
  Simple3D | transform direction X:((<key (d v) pressed?> - <key (a v) pressed?>) * (0.5)) Y:(0) Z:((<key (s v) pressed?> - <key (w v) pressed?>) * (0.5)) from [view space v] to [world space v]

end
Simple3D | move X:(() - (camX)) Y:(() - (camY)) Z:(() - (camZ))

define draw

define gen cone (steps) (radius) (length)
delete all of [coneX v]
delete all of [coneY v]
delete all of [coneZ v]
Simple3D | configure [custom v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
add [0] to [coneX v]
add [0] to [coneY v]
add [0] to [coneZ v]
repeat ((steps) + (1))
  Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((360) / (steps)) degrees
  Simple3D | transform X:(radius) Y:((0) - (length)) Z:(0)
  add (Simple3D | transformed [X v]) to [coneX v]
  add (Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) to [coneY v]
  add (Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) to [coneZ v]
end

define Create plane
Simple3D | create mesh [plane]
Simple3D | set [plane] positions XYZ [planeX v] [planeY v] [planeZ v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture coordinates UV [planeU v] [planeV v]
Simple3D | set [plane] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [plane] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (grass v)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture mipmapping [smooth transitions v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture anisotropic filtering (16 v)
Simple3D | set [plane] blending (overwrite color (fastest for opaque) v)

define Create cone
gen cone [50] [20] [50] :: custom
Simple3D | create mesh [cone]
Simple3D | set [cone] positions XYZ [coneX v] [coneY v] [coneZ v]
Simple3D | set [cone] primitives (triangle fan v)
Simple3D | create mesh [coneAdd]
Simple3D | make [coneAdd] inherit from meshes [cone]
Simple3D | set [coneAdd] blending (additive v)
Simple3D | set [coneAdd] cull (back faces v)
Simple3D | create mesh [coneSub]
Simple3D | make [coneSub] inherit from meshes [cone]
Simple3D | set [coneSub] blending (subtractive v)
Simple3D | set [coneSub] cull (front faces v)

define Create boxes
Simple3D | create mesh [box]
Simple3D | set [box] indices [boxIdx v]
Simple3D | set [box] positions XYZ [boxX v] [boxY v] [boxZ v]
Simple3D | set [box] texture coordinates UV [boxU v] [boxV v]
Simple3D | set [box] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [box] cull (back faces v)
Simple3D | set [box] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (2x2 v)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [box] blending (overwrite color (fastest for opaque) v)
Simple3D | set [box] accurate interpolation (true v)
delete all of [boxTr v]
delete all of [boxUV v]
repeat (1000)
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | move X:(pick random (-100) to (100)) Y:(pick random (2) to (6)) Z:(pick random (-100) to (100))
  Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (pick random (-180) to (180)) degrees
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (pick random (-180) to (180)) degrees
  Simple3D | save into [boxTr v] at ((length of [boxTr v]) + (1))
  add ((pick random (0) to (1)) * (0.5)) to [boxUV v]
  add ((pick random (0) to (1)) * (0.5)) to [boxUV v]
  add [0.5] to [boxUV v]
  add [0.5] to [boxUV v]
end
Simple3D | set [box] instance [transforms v] [boxTr v]
Simple3D | set [box] instance [UV offsets and sizes v] [boxUV v]

define Draw plane

Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | scale X:(200) Y:(100) Z:(200)
Simple3D | draw mesh [plane]

define Draw box

Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | draw mesh [box]

define Create lights (amount)
delete all of [lights v]
repeat ((amount) * (7))
  add (pick random (-100.0) to (100.0)) to [lights v]
end

define Setup fog
Simple3D | turn fog (true v)
Simple3D | set fog distance near:(141) far:(145)
Simple3D | set fog color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0.1)
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0.1) A:(1)
Simple3D | set fog world space origin at X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(0)

define Create glow
Simple3D | create mesh [glow]
Simple3D | set [glow] positions XY [planeX v] [planeZ v]
Simple3D | set [glow] texture coordinates UV [glowU v] [glowV v]
Simple3D | set [glow] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [glow] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (yellow glow v)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [glow] blending (additive v)
Simple3D | set [glow] billboarding (true v)

define Draw glow
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1) G:(1) B:(1) A:(1)
set [light v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [lights v]) / (7))
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | move X:(item ((light) + (1)) of [lights v]) Y:(0) Z:(item ((light) + (2)) of [lights v])
  Simple3D | move X:((7) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (3)) of [lights v])) * (23)))) Y:((20) + ((5) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (4)) of [lights v])) * (30))))) Z:((3) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (5)) of [lights v])) * (37))))
  Simple3D | scale X:(2) Y:(2) Z:(2)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [glow]
  change [light v] by (7)
end

define Draw lights
set [light v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [lights v]) / (7))
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | move X:(item ((light) + (1)) of [lights v]) Y:(0) Z:(item ((light) + (2)) of [lights v])
  Simple3D | move X:((7) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (3)) of [lights v])) * (23)))) Y:((20) + ((5) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (4)) of [lights v])) * (30))))) Z:((3) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (5)) of [lights v])) * (37))))
  Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (((30) * ([sin v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (6)) of [lights v])) * (41)))) + ((30) * ([cos v] of (((timer) + (item ((light) + (7)) of [lights v])) * (27))))) degrees
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (30) degrees
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.5) G:(0.4) B:(0.1) A:(0.5)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [coneAdd]
  Simple3D | transform X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(0) from [view space v] to [model space v]
  if <<(Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) < [0]> and <([sqrt v] of (((Simple3D | transformed [X v]) * (Simple3D | transformed [X v])) + ((Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) * (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])))) < (((Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) / (-50)) * (20))>> then
    Simple3D | wrapper {
      Simple3D | depth test (everything v) write (false v)
      Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
      Simple3D | start with no transformation
      Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
      Simple3D | start with no transformation
      Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
      Simple3D | start with no transformation
      Simple3D | draw mesh [fullscreen]
      Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (false v)
    }
  end
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.5) G:(0.4) B:(0.1) A:(0.45)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [coneSub]
  change [light v] by (7)
end

Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.1) G:(0.4) B:(0.5) A:(0.5)
Simple3D | draw mesh [coneAdd]
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.1) G:(0.4) B:(0.5) A:(0.45)
Simple3D | draw mesh [coneSub]
change [light v] by (7)

define Create fullscreen
Simple3D | create mesh [fullscreen]
Simple3D | set [fullscreen] positions XY [planeX v] [planeZ v]
Simple3D | set [fullscreen] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [fullscreen] blending (additive v)

define Create windows
delete all of [aWindowMinX v]
delete all of [aWindowMinY v]
delete all of [aWindowMaxX v]
delete all of [aWindowMaxY v]
delete all of [aWindowOrder v]
delete all of [camData v]
Simple3D | create mesh [windowBase]
Simple3D | set [windowBase] positions XY [windowX v] [windowY v]
Simple3D | set [windowBase] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | create mesh [windowBaseUV]
Simple3D | make [windowBaseUV] inherit from meshes [windowBase]
Simple3D | set [windowBaseUV] texture coordinates UV [windowU v] [windowV v]
Simple3D | create mesh [windowClose]
Simple3D | make [windowClose] inherit from meshes [windowBaseUV]
Simple3D | set [windowClose] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume x)) [clamp to edge v] [blurred v]
set [i v] to [0]
repeat (windowCount)
  change [i v] by (1)
  add (pick random (-240) to (-20)) to [aWindowMinX v]
  add (pick random (-180) to (-20)) to [aWindowMinY v]
  add (pick random (240) to (20)) to [aWindowMaxX v]
  add (pick random (150) to (20)) to [aWindowMaxY v]
  add (i) to [aWindowOrder v]
  add (pick random (-100) to (100)) to [camData v]
  add (pick random (2) to (10)) to [camData v]
  add (pick random (-100) to (100)) to [camData v]
  add (pick random (-45) to (45)) to [camData v]
  add (pick random (-180) to (180)) to [camData v]
  Simple3D | create mesh (join [window] (i))
  Simple3D | make (join [window] (i)) inherit from meshes [windowBaseUV]
  resize window texture (i) :: custom
end

define draw all
set [i v] to [0]
repeat (windowCount)
  change [i v] by (1)
  Load cam data (i) :: custom
  controls :: custom
  Simple3D | render to texture of (join [window] (i))
  draw :: custom
end

define draw windows
Simple3D | render to stage
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Simple3D | depth test (everything v) write (false v)
Simple3D | wrapper {
  Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | scale X:((2) / (runtimeoptions | stage (width v))) Y:((2) / (runtimeoptions | stage (height v))) Z:(1)
  set [j v] to (windowCount)
  repeat (windowCount)
    set [i v] to (item (j) of [aWindowOrder v])
    if <(i) = (windowI)> then
      Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0.25) G:(0.5) B:(1) A:(1)
    end
    drawRectangle ((item (i) of [aWindowMinX v]) - (1)) ((item (i) of [aWindowMinY v]) - (1)) ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxX v]) + (1)) ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v]) + (20)) [windowBase] :: custom
    Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1) G:(1) B:(1) A:(1)
    drawRectangle ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxX v]) - (15)) ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v]) + (4)) ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxX v]) - (3)) ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v]) + (16)) [windowClose] :: custom
    drawRectangle (item (i) of [aWindowMinX v]) (item (i) of [aWindowMinY v]) (item (i) of [aWindowMaxX v]) (item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v]) (join [window] (i)) :: custom
    change [j v] by (-1)
  end
}

define drawRectangle (x1) (y1) (x2) (y2) (mesh)
Simple3D | wrapper {
  Simple3D | move X:(x1) Y:(y1) Z:(0)
  Simple3D | scale X:((x2) - (x1)) Y:((y2) - (y1)) Z:(1)
  Simple3D | draw mesh (mesh)
}

define Change cursor
if <not <pointerlock | pointer locked?>> then
  if <(pointer lock) = [1]> then
    pointerlock | set pointer lock (false v)
    set [pointer lock v] to [0]
  end
end
if <<not <mouse down?>> and <not <pointerlock | pointer locked?>>> then
  set [cursor v] to [default]
  scan windows :: custom
  if <not <MouseCursor | cursor = (cursor)>> then
    MouseCursor | set cursor to (cursor)
  end
else
  if <<(windowI) > [0]> and <not <(item (1) of [aWindowOrder v]) = (windowI)>>> then
    delete (item # of (windowI) in [aWindowOrder v]) of [aWindowOrder v]
    insert (windowI) at (1) of [aWindowOrder v]
  end
  if <(mode) = [focus]> then
    set [pointer lock v] to [1]
    pointerlock | set pointer lock (true v)
  end
  if <(mode) = [drag]> then
    set [dx v] to ((mouse x) - (last mouse X))
    set [dy v] to ((mouse y) - (last mouse Y))
    replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMaxX v] with ((item (windowI) of [aWindowMaxX v]) + (dx))
    replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMaxY v] with ((item (windowI) of [aWindowMaxY v]) + (dy))
    replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMinX v] with ((item (windowI) of [aWindowMinX v]) + (dx))
    replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMinY v] with ((item (windowI) of [aWindowMinY v]) + (dy))
  end
  if <(mode) = [resize]> then
    if <(mouse X dir) = [w]> then
      replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMinX v] with (mouse x)
    end
    if <(mouse X dir) = [e]> then
      replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMaxX v] with (mouse x)
    end
    if <(mouse Y dir) = [s]> then
      replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMinY v] with (mouse y)
    end
    if <(mouse Y dir) = [n]> then
      replace item (windowI) of [aWindowMaxY v] with ((mouse y) - (20))
    end
    resize window texture (windowI) :: custom
  end
end
set [last mouse X v] to (mouse x)
set [last mouse Y v] to (mouse y)

(pointer v)

define scan windows
set [windowI v] to [0]
set [mode v] to [none]
set [j v] to [0]
repeat (windowCount)
  change [j v] by (1)
  set [i v] to (item (j) of [aWindowOrder v])
  if <<<(mouse x) > ((item (i) of [aWindowMinX v]) - (3))> and <(mouse x) < ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxX v]) + (3))>> and <<(mouse y) > ((item (i) of [aWindowMinY v]) - (3))> and <(mouse y) < ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v]) + (21))>>> then
    set [mouse X dir v] to []
    set [mouse Y dir v] to []
    if <([abs v] of ((mouse x) - (item (i) of [aWindowMinX v]))) < [2]> then
      set [mouse X dir v] to [w]
    end
    if <([abs v] of ((mouse x) - (item (i) of [aWindowMaxX v]))) < [2]> then
      set [mouse X dir v] to [e]
    end
    if <([abs v] of ((mouse y) - (item (i) of [aWindowMinY v]))) < [2]> then
      set [mouse Y dir v] to [s]
    end
    if <([abs v] of ((mouse y) - ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v]) + (19)))) < [2]> then
      set [mouse Y dir v] to [n]
    end
    set [mode v] to [focus]
    if <(mouse y) > (item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v])> then
      set [mode v] to [drag]
      set [cursor v] to [grab]
    end
    if <not <(join (mouse Y dir) (mouse X dir)) = []>> then
      set [mode v] to [resize]
      set [cursor v] to (join (join (mouse Y dir) (mouse X dir)) [-resize])
    end
    set [windowI v] to (i)
    stop [this script v]
  end
end

define resize window texture (i)
Simple3D | set (join [window] (i)) texture (Simple3D | texture of size ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxX v]) - (item (i) of [aWindowMinX v])) ((item (i) of [aWindowMaxY v]) - (item (i) of [aWindowMinY v]))) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]

define Load cam data

define Load cam data (id)
set [camX v] to (item (((id) * (5)) - (4)) of [camData v])
set [camY v] to (item (((id) * (5)) - (3)) of [camData v])
set [camZ v] to (item (((id) * (5)) - (2)) of [camData v])
set [camRotX v] to (item (((id) * (5)) - (1)) of [camData v])
set [camRotY v] to (item ((id) * (5)) of [camData v])

define Save cam data (id)
replace item (((id) * (5)) - (4)) of [camData v] with (camX)
replace item (((id) * (5)) - (3)) of [camData v] with (camY)
replace item (((id) * (5)) - (2)) of [camData v] with (camZ)
replace item (((id) * (5)) - (1)) of [camData v] with (camRotX)
replace item ((id) * (5)) of [camData v] with (camRotY)

// Textures are drawn by Vadik1/Xeltalliv and licensed under CC BY-SA 4.0.\n(CC BY-SA 4.0 is not compatible with scratch website)\nThis cobblestone texture have been posted (and became widespread, with most people either not crediting me, or falsely crediting minecraft) on scratch, so it's CC BY-SA 2.0.\n\nDo whatever you want with the code.

```

### Blocks

```scratchblocks
when flag clicked

  broadcast (System: Tick v) and wait

set [Camera: X v] to [-215]
```

```scratchblocks
delete all of [_uploadI v]
delete all of [_uploadW v]
Read from [Vertex] :: custom // Loading points
set [s1 v] to [0]
repeat ((length of [_tmp v]) / (3))
  change [s1 v] by (3)
  Add Point ((item ((s1) - (2)) of [_tmp v]) * (250)) ((item (s1) of [_tmp v]) * (250)) ((item ((s1) - (1)) of [_tmp v]) * (-250)) :: custom
end
Read from [Colors] :: custom // Loading color data
delete all of [_colors v]
repeat (length of [_tmp v])
  add (item (1) of [_tmp v]) to [_colors v]
  delete (1) of [_tmp v]
end
Read from [NoCull] :: custom // Disabling backface culling for certain polys
repeat (length of [_tmp v])
  replace item (item (1) of [_tmp v]) of [Surfaces: NoCull v] with [1]
  delete (1) of [_tmp v]
end
delete all of [_tmp2 v]
delete all of [_tmp v] // Loading weights and connections of points to joints
set [s5 v] to [0]
repeat (length of [JointNames v])
  change [s5 v] by (1)
  Load connections for (item (s5) of [JointNames v]) (s5) :: custom
end
set [s1 v] to [0] // Scaling weights so that their summ is 1\n
repeat (length of [Point Data: 1I v])
  change [s1 v] by (1)
  set [s2 v] to ((item (s1) of [Point Data: 1W v]) + ((item (s1) of [Point Data: 2W v]) + (item (s1) of [Point Data: 3W v])))
  if <(s2) = [0]> then
    stop [all v]
  else
    replace item (s1) of [Point Data: 1W v] with ((item (s1) of [Point Data: 1W v]) / (s2))
    replace item (s1) of [Point Data: 2W v] with ((item (s1) of [Point Data: 2W v]) / (s2))
    replace item (s1) of [Point Data: 3W v] with ((item (s1) of [Point Data: 3W v]) / (s2))
  end // Fail check
end
Read from [VertexIndex] :: custom // Loading surfaces
set [s2 v] to [1]
set [s1 v] to [1]
repeat until <not <(s1) < (length of [_tmp v])>>
  if <(item ((s1) + (2)) of [_tmp v]) < [0]> then
    Add Triangle Surface ((item (s1) of [_tmp v]) + (1)) ((item ((s1) + (1)) of [_tmp v]) + (1)) ((0) - (item ((s1) + (2)) of [_tmp v])) Color (item (s2) of [_colors v]) (item ((s2) + (1)) of [_colors v]) (item ((s2) + (2)) of [_colors v]) :: custom
    change [s1 v] by (3)
  else
    Add Triangle Surface ((item (s1) of [_tmp v]) + (1)) ((item ((s1) + (1)) of [_tmp v]) + (1)) ((item ((s1) + (2)) of [_tmp v]) + (1)) Color (item (s2) of [_colors v]) (item ((s2) + (1)) of [_colors v]) (item ((s2) + (2)) of [_colors v]) :: custom
    Add Triangle Surface ((item (s1) of [_tmp v]) + (1)) ((item ((s1) + (2)) of [_tmp v]) + (1)) ((0) - (item ((s1) + (3)) of [_tmp v])) Color (item (s2) of [_colors v]) (item ((s2) + (1)) of [_colors v]) (item ((s2) + (2)) of [_colors v]) :: custom
    change [s1 v] by (4)
  end
  change [s2 v] by (3)
end
set [s5 v] to [0] // Loading bones
Simple3D | configure [custom v] transformation
repeat (length of [JointNames v])
  change [s5 v] by (1)
  Load bone data (item (s5) of [JointNames v]) (item (s5) of [JointParent v]) :: custom
end

Import Model :: custom

define Read numbers from (line) (char)
set [s3 v] to []
delete all of [_tmp v]
set [s1 v] to (line)
forever
  set [s2 v] to (((char) - (1)) * <(s1) = (line)>)
  repeat ((length of (item (s1) of [IMPORT v])) - (((char) - (1)) * <(s1) = (line)>))
    change [s2 v] by (1)
    set [s4 v] to (letter (s2) of (item (s1) of [IMPORT v]))
    if <(s4) = [!]> then
      add (s3) to [_tmp v]
      stop [this script v]
    else
      if <(s4) = [ ]> then
        if <(length of (s3)) > [0]> then
          add (s3) to [_tmp v]
          set [s3 v] to []
        end
      else
        if <<(s4) = ([abs v] of (s4))> or <<(s4) = [.]> or <(s4) = [-]>>> then
          set [s3 v] to (join (s3) (s4))
        end
      end
    end
  end
  change [s1 v] by (1)
end

define Read from (word)
Read numbers from (item # of (join [!] (word)) in [IMPORT v]) [2] :: custom

define Read from (word) w/offset (l)
Read numbers from ((item # of (join [!] (word)) in [IMPORT v]) + (l)) [2] :: custom

(ch)

define Load connections for (name) (id)
Read from (name) w/offset [6] :: custom
delete all of [_tmp2 v]
repeat (length of [_tmp v])
  add (item (1) of [_tmp v]) to [_tmp2 v]
  delete (1) of [_tmp v]
end
Read from (name) w/offset [5] :: custom
repeat (length of [_tmp v])
  set [s3 v] to ((item (1) of [_tmp v]) + (1))
  set [s1 v] to [1]
  set [s2 v] to (item (s3) of [Point Data: 1W v])
  if <(item (s3) of [Point Data: 2W v]) < (s2)> then
    set [s1 v] to [2]
    set [s2 v] to (item (s3) of [Point Data: 2W v])
  end
  if <(item (s3) of [Point Data: 3W v]) < (s2)> then
    set [s1 v] to [3]
    set [s2 v] to (item (s3) of [Point Data: 3W v])
  end
  if <(item (1) of [_tmp2 v]) > (s2)> then
    if <(s1) = [1]> then
      replace item (s3) of [Point Data: 1I v] with (id)
      replace item (s3) of [Point Data: 1W v] with (item (1) of [_tmp2 v])
    else
      if <(s1) = [2]> then
        replace item (s3) of [Point Data: 2I v] with (id)
        replace item (s3) of [Point Data: 2W v] with (item (1) of [_tmp2 v])
      else
        replace item (s3) of [Point Data: 3I v] with (id)
        replace item (s3) of [Point Data: 3W v] with (item (1) of [_tmp2 v])
      end
    end
  end
  delete (1) of [_tmp v]
  delete (1) of [_tmp2 v]
end

define Load bone data (name) (parent)
Read from (name) w/offset [1] :: custom
set [posX v] to ((item (1) of [_tmp v]) * (250))
set [posY v] to ((item (2) of [_tmp v]) * (250))
set [posZ v] to ((item (3) of [_tmp v]) * (250))
Read from (name) w/offset [2] :: custom
set [rotX v] to (item (1) of [_tmp v])
set [rotY v] to (item (2) of [_tmp v])
set [rotZ v] to (item (3) of [_tmp v])
if <(parent) > [0]> then
  Simple3D | start with saved in [JointOrigAbsMat v] at ((((parent) - (1)) * (16)) + (1))
else
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (-90) degrees
end
Simple3D | move X:(posX) Y:(posY) Z:(posZ)
Simple3D | rotate around [Z v] by (rotZ) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (rotY) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (rotX) degrees
Simple3D | save into [JointOrigAbsMat v] at ((length of [JointOrigAbsMat v]) + (1))
Begin Object (posX) (posY) (posZ) (rotX) (rotY) (rotZ) :: custom
End Object :: custom

```

```scratchblocks
Calculate bone matrices :: custom

define Calculate bone matrices
Simple3D | configure [custom v] transformation
delete all of [JointNewAbsMat v]
set [s5 v] to [0] // Calculating matrices the same way we did at initiation stage.\n\nThen multiplying new matrices by inverted old ones to get a matrix telling how to get from old to new\n\nOldToNew = New * inv(Old)
repeat (length of [JointNames v])
  change [s5 v] by (1)
  Process bone ((s5) + (1)) (item (s5) of [JointParent v]) :: custom
end
Simple3D | set [wyvern] (current v) transforms [JointNewAbsMat v]

define Process bone (id) (parent)
if <(parent) > [0]> then
  Simple3D | start with saved in [JointNewAbsMat v] at ((((parent) - (1)) * (16)) + (1))
else
  Simple3D | start with no transformation
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (-90) degrees
end
```

## Sprite: "UI"

### Costumes
1. "line" - slider base line
2. "slider" - slider draggable part
3. "next" - button with right arrow
4. "edit" - edit button
5. "close" - close button
6. "prev" - button with left arrow
7. "bg" - rectangle that serves as background for ui
8. "text" - 'x rotation' 'y rotation' 'z rotation' labels for sliders
9. "credits" - 'Model: https://opengameart.org/content/wyvern-1'
10. "I Like Lag" - 'Run on scratch anyways' button

### Sounds
1. "pop" - default scratch pop sound

### Local Variables
_type = "orig"
_extra = "edit"

### Blocks
```scratchblocks
define UI (x) (y) (s) (c) (t) (e)
go to x: (x) y: (y)
set size to (s) %
switch costume to (c)
set [_type v] to (t)
set [_extra v] to (e)
create clone of (_myself_ v)
set [_type v] to [orig]

when I start as a clone
show
if <(_type) = [bg]> then
  set [GHOST v] effect to (50)
end

when this sprite clicked
if <(_type) = [sl]> then
  repeat until <not <mouse down?>>
    if <(mouse x) < [-222]> then
      set x to (-222)
    else
      if <(mouse x) > [-138]> then
        set x to (-138)
      else
        set x to (mouse x)
      end
    end
    if <(_extra) = [x]> then
      replace item ((Selected) + (1)) of [Objects: Rot X v] with (((x position) - (-180)) / (0.2333))
    end
    if <(_extra) = [y]> then
      replace item ((Selected) + (1)) of [Objects: Rot Y v] with (((x position) - (-180)) / (0.2333))
    end
    if <(_extra) = [z]> then
      replace item ((Selected) + (1)) of [Objects: Rot Z v] with (((x position) - (-180)) / (0.2333))
    end
  end
end
if <(_type) = [b]> then
  if <(_extra) = [next]> then
    set [Selected v] to (((Selected) mod (length of [JointNames v])) + (1))
    broadcast (Update UI v)
  end
  if <(_extra) = [prev]> then
    set [Selected v] to ((((Selected) - (2)) mod (length of [JointNames v])) + (1))
    broadcast (Update UI v)
  end
  if <(_extra) = [close]> then
    hide variable [Type v]
    set [Type v] to []
    broadcast (Update UI v)
  end
  if <(_extra) = [edit]> then
    show variable [Type v]
    set [Type v] to [Loading]
    Make UI :: custom
    delete this clone
  end
end

when I receive [Init: UI v]
broadcast (Update UI v)

when I receive [Update UI v]
if <(Type) = []> then
  if <not <>> then
    delete this clone
  end
  UI [-226] [166] [200] [edit] [b] [edit] :: custom
else
  if <(_type) = [orig]> then
    set [Type v] to (item (Selected) of [JointNames v])
  end
  if <(_type) = [sl]> then
    if <(_extra) = [x]> then
      set [_type v] to (item ((Selected) + (1)) of [Objects: Rot X v])
    end
    if <(_extra) = [y]> then
      set [_type v] to (item ((Selected) + (1)) of [Objects: Rot Y v])
    end
    if <(_extra) = [z]> then
      set [_type v] to (item ((Selected) + (1)) of [Objects: Rot Z v])
    end
    set x to ((-180) + ((_type) * (0.2333)))
    set [_type v] to [sl]
  end
end

define Make UI
UI [-180] [100] [300] [bg] [bg] [] :: custom
UI [-180] [100] [300] [text] [bg] [] :: custom
UI [0] [0] [100] [credits] [bg] [] :: custom
UI [-224] [136] [200] [prev] [b] [prev] :: custom
UI [-200] [136] [200] [next] [b] [next] :: custom
UI [-156] [136] [200] [close] [b] [close] :: custom
UI [-180] [100] [100] [line] [bg] [x] :: custom
UI [-180] [100] [100] [slider] [sl] [x] :: custom
UI [-180] [70] [100] [line] [bg] [y] :: custom
UI [-180] [70] [100] [slider] [sl] [y] :: custom
UI [-180] [40] [100] [line] [bg] [z] :: custom
UI [-180] [40] [100] [slider] [sl] [z] :: custom
broadcast (Update UI v)

when flag clicked
erase all
hide variable [Type v]
set [Selected v] to [1]
set [_type v] to [orig]
set [Type v] to []
hide

```

```scratchblocks
Draw room (prefix) :: custom
Draw susanne (prefix) :: custom
Draw teapot (prefix) :: custom

define Draw room normally
Simple3D | render to stage
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1.5) G:(1.5) B:(1.5) A:(1)
Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(0)
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Draw everything [] :: custom
Simple3D | depth test (everything v) write (false v)
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | draw mesh [light]

define Draw lights
Simple3D | set fog color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0)
Simple3D | render to texture of [light]
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(0.2) G:(0.2) B:(0.2) A:(0)
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Draw everything [] :: custom
Simple3D | turn fog (true v)
Simple3D | depth test (same v) write (false v)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
set [i v] to [0]
repeat (length of [lightB v])
  change [i v] by (1)
  Simple3D | set fog distance near:((item (i) of [lightI v]) * (0.2)) far:((item (i) of [lightI v]) * (1.3))
  Simple3D | set fog world space origin at X:(item (i) of [lightX v]) Y:(item (i) of [lightY v]) Z:(item (i) of [lightZ v])
  Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(item (i) of [lightR v]) G:(item (i) of [lightG v]) B:(item (i) of [lightB v]) A:(0)
  Draw everything [a_] :: custom
end
Simple3D | turn fog (false v)

define Clear lights
delete all of [lightX v]
delete all of [lightY v]
delete all of [lightZ v]
delete all of [lightR v]
delete all of [lightG v]
delete all of [lightB v]
delete all of [lightI v]

define add light (x) (y) (z) color (r) (g) (b) intensity (i)
add (x) to [lightX v]
add (y) to [lightY v]
add (z) to [lightZ v]
add (r) to [lightR v]
add (g) to [lightG v]
add (b) to [lightB v]
add (i) to [lightI v]

define Create meshes
Simple3D | create mesh [room]
Simple3D | set [room] positions XYZ [roomX v] [roomY v] [roomZ v]
Simple3D | set [room] texture coordinates UV [roomU v] [roomV v]
Simple3D | set [room] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume sprite_30)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | create mesh [teapot]
Simple3D | set [teapot] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume test)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [teapot] from [obj mtl v] [teapot v]
Simple3D | create mesh [susanne]
Simple3D | set [susanne] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume test)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [susanne] from [obj mtl v] [susanne v]
Simple3D | create mesh [light]
Simple3D | set [light] positions XY [x v] [y v]
Simple3D | set [light] texture coordinates UV [u v] [v v]
Simple3D | set [light] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [light] texture (Simple3D | texture of size (Simple3D | canvas width) (Simple3D | canvas height)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [light] blending (multiply v)
Simple3D | create mesh [a_room]
Simple3D | create mesh [a_teapot]
Simple3D | create mesh [a_susanne]
Simple3D | make [a_room] inherit from meshes [room]
Simple3D | make [a_teapot] inherit from meshes [teapot]
Simple3D | make [a_susanne] inherit from meshes [susanne]
Simple3D | set [a_room] blending (additive v)
Simple3D | set [a_teapot] blending (additive v)
Simple3D | set [a_susanne] blending (additive v)
```

```scratchblocks
define Draw
if <(prefix) = []> then
  Simple3D | render to stage
else
  Simple3D | render to texture of [room2]
end
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000)
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (() - (camRotX)) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (() - (camRotY)) degrees
Simple3D | move X:(() - (camX)) Y:(() - (camY)) Z:(() - (camZ))
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
Draw room normally :: custom
Draw lights :: custom
No transforms :: custom
Combine lights :: custom
Apply combined lights :: custom

define Draw everything
Draw room :: custom
Draw susanne :: custom
Draw teapot :: custom

define Draw room normally
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1.5) G:(1.5) B:(1.5) A:(1)
Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(0)
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Draw everything :: custom

define Draw lights
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
Simple3D | set fog color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0)
Simple3D | turn fog (true v)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
set [i v] to [0]
repeat (length of [lightB v])
  change [i v] by (1)
  Simple3D | render to texture of (join (prefix) (join [light] (i)))
  Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
  Simple3D | set fog distance near:((item (i) of [lightI v]) * (0.2)) far:((item (i) of [lightI v]) * (1.3))
  Simple3D | set fog world space origin at X:(item (i) of [lightX v]) Y:(item (i) of [lightY v]) Z:(item (i) of [lightZ v])
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
  Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(item (i) of [lightR v]) G:(item (i) of [lightG v]) B:(item (i) of [lightB v]) A:(0)
  Draw everything :: custom
end
Simple3D | turn fog (false v)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1) G:(1) B:(1) A:(1)
Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(0)

define Clear lights
delete all of [lightX v]
delete all of [lightY v]
delete all of [lightZ v]
delete all of [lightR v]
delete all of [lightG v]
delete all of [lightB v]
delete all of [lightI v]

define add light (x) (y) (z) color (r) (g) (b) intensity (i)
add (x) to [lightX v]
add (y) to [lightY v]
add (z) to [lightZ v]
add (r) to [lightR v]
add (g) to [lightG v]
add (b) to [lightB v]
add (i) to [lightI v]

define Create meshes
Simple3D | create mesh [room]
Simple3D | set [room] positions XYZ [roomX v] [roomY v] [roomZ v]
Simple3D | set [room] texture coordinates UV [roomU v] [roomV v]
Simple3D | set [room] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume sprite_30)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | create mesh [room2]
Simple3D | make [room2] inherit from meshes [room]
Simple3D | set [room2] texture (Simple3D | texture of size (256) (256)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | create mesh [teapot]
Simple3D | set [teapot] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume test)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [teapot] from [obj mtl v] [teapot v]
Simple3D | create mesh [susanne]
Simple3D | set [susanne] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume test)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [susanne] from [obj mtl v] [susanne v]
Simple3D | create mesh [lightBase]
Simple3D | set [lightBase] positions XY [x v] [y v]
Simple3D | set [lightBase] texture coordinates UV [u v] [v v]
Simple3D | set [lightBase] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | create mesh [lightCombined]
Simple3D | make [lightCombined] inherit from meshes [lightBase]
Simple3D | set [lightCombined] texture (Simple3D | texture of size (Simple3D | canvas width) (Simple3D | canvas height)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [lightCombined] blending (multiply v)
Simple3D | create mesh [sqrlightCombined]
Simple3D | make [sqrlightCombined] inherit from meshes [lightBase]
Simple3D | set [sqrlightCombined] texture (Simple3D | texture of size (256) (256)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [sqrlightCombined] blending (multiply v)
set [i v] to [0]
repeat (length of [lightI v])
  change [i v] by (1)
  Simple3D | create mesh (join [light] (i))
  Simple3D | make (join [light] (i)) inherit from meshes [lightBase]
  Simple3D | set (join [light] (i)) texture (Simple3D | texture of size (Simple3D | canvas width) (Simple3D | canvas height)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
  Simple3D | set (join [light] (i)) blending (additive v)
  Simple3D | render to texture of (join [light] (i))
  Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
  Simple3D | create mesh (join [sqrlight] (i))
  Simple3D | make (join [sqrlight] (i)) inherit from meshes [lightBase]
  Simple3D | set (join [sqrlight] (i)) texture (Simple3D | texture of size (256) (256)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
  Simple3D | set (join [sqrlight] (i)) blending (additive v)
  Simple3D | render to texture of (join [sqrlight] (i))
  Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
end
Simple3D | render to stage

define Resize lights
Simple3D | set [lightCombined] texture (Simple3D | texture of size (Simple3D | canvas width) (Simple3D | canvas height)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
set [i v] to [0]
repeat (length of [lightI v])
  change [i v] by (1)
  Simple3D | set (join [light] (i)) texture (Simple3D | texture of size (Simple3D | canvas width) (Simple3D | canvas height)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
end

define No transforms
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation

define Combine lights
Simple3D | render to texture of (join (prefix) [lightCombined])
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0.2) G:(0.2) B:(0.2) A:(1)
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
set [i v] to [0]
repeat (length of [lightB v])
  change [i v] by (1)
  Simple3D | draw mesh (join (prefix) (join [light] (i)))
end
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)

define Apply combined lights
if <(prefix) = []> then
  Simple3D | render to stage
else
  Simple3D | render to texture of [room2]
end
if <key (space v) pressed?> then
  Simple3D | set clear color R:(1) G:(1) B:(1) A:(1)
  Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
end
Simple3D | depth test (everything v) write (false v)
Simple3D | draw mesh (join (prefix) [lightCombined])

set [prefix v] to []
```

# Project: Walking 1

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grid" - a 16x16 png containing a 2x2 white/grey checkerboard pattern
2. "box" - a 16x16 png of stereotypical box, with border around the edge and X-shape in the middle

### Blocks
```scratchblocks
when flag clicked
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
Simple3D | create mesh [plane]
Simple3D | set [plane] positions XY [planeX v] [planeY v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture coordinates UV [planeU v] [planeV v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume grid)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture mipmapping [smooth transitions v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture anisotropic filtering (16 v)
Simple3D | create mesh [box]
Simple3D | set [box] indices [boxIdx v]
Simple3D | set [box] positions XYZ [boxX v] [boxY v] [boxZ v]
Simple3D | set [box] texture coordinates UV [boxU v] [boxV v]
Simple3D | set [box] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [box] texture () [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [box] discord pixels less opaque than [0.1], for those that pass true
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000)
forever
  controls :: custom
  Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
  Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
  draw plane :: custom
  draw boxes :: custom
end

(Simple3D | texture from costume (costume box))

define controls
if <key (c v) pressed?> then
  set [camRotX v] to (mouse y)
  set [camRotY v] to ((0) - (mouse x))
end
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation

Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((0) - (camRotY)) degrees
Simple3D | transform direction X:(<key (d v) pressed?> - <key (a v) pressed?>) Y:(0) Z:(<key (s v) pressed?> - <key (w v) pressed?>) from [view space v] to [world space v]
change [camX v] by (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
change [camY v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
change [camZ v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])

Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000) // This block implicitly takes aspect ratio of the currently selected render target.

draw box at [-4] [0] [-2] size [8] [12] [4] :: custom

  Simple3D | move X:(0) Y:(12) Z:(0)
  Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (([sin v] of (timer)) * (2)) degrees
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (([sin v] of (((timer) * (2)) - (90))) * (3)) degrees
  draw box at [-4] [0] [-4] size [8] [8] [8] :: custom
}

  Simple3D | move X:(-6) Y:(10) Z:(0)

  draw box at [-2] [-4] [-2] size [4] [6] [4] :: custom

    }
  }
}

  Simple3D | move X:(6) Y:(10) Z:(0)
  Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by ((10) + (([sin v] of (timer)) * (45))) degrees

  Simple3D | move X:(-2) Y:(0) Z:(0)

  draw box at [-2] [-6] [-2] size [4] [6] [4] :: custom

    Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by ((20) + (([sin v] of ((timer) + (110))) * (-20))) degrees

  }

    Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by ((20) + (([sin v] of ((timer) + (110))) * (20))) degrees

define controls (6 directions)
set [camRotX v] to (mouse y)
set [camRotY v] to ((0) - (mouse x))

change [camY v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])

  Simple3D | move X:(x) Y:(y) Z:(z)
  Simple3D | scale X:(sx) Y:(sy) Z:(sz)
```

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grid" - a 16x16 png containing a 2x2 white/grey checkerboard pattern
2. "box" - a 16x16 png of stereotypical box, with border around the edge and X-shape in the middle

### Blocks
```scratchblocks
when flag clicked
pointerlock | set pointer lock (true v)
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(1)
Simple3D | create mesh [plane]
Simple3D | set [plane] positions XY [planeX v] [planeY v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture coordinates UV [planeU v] [planeV v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume grid)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture mipmapping [smooth transitions v]
Simple3D | set [plane] texture anisotropic filtering (16 v)
Simple3D | create mesh [box]
Simple3D | set [box] indices [boxIdx v]
Simple3D | set [box] positions XYZ [boxX v] [boxY v] [boxZ v]
Simple3D | set [box] texture coordinates UV [boxU v] [boxV v]
Simple3D | set [box] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [box] discord pixels less opaque than [0.1], for those that pass true
Simple3D | set [box] cull (back faces v)
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.01) far: (1000)
set [timer v] to [0]
forever
  controls :: custom
  Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
  Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
  draw plane :: custom
  draw boxes :: custom
end

(Simple3D | texture from costume (costume box))

define controls
set [walkX v] to (<key (d v) pressed?> - <key (a v) pressed?>)
set [walkZ v] to (<key (s v) pressed?> - <key (w v) pressed?>)
set [walkLength v] to ([sqrt v] of (((walkX) * (walkX)) + ((walkZ) * (walkZ))))
```

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grid" - a 16x16 png containing a 2x2 white/grey checkerboard pattern
2. "box" - a 16x16 png of stereotypical box, with border around the edge and X-shape in the middle
3. "uv grid" - a 256x256 png of horizontal left-to-right 0-to-255 red gradient, vertical top-to-bottom 0-to-255 green gradient, and a 4x4 grid on top of the gradient. Top left corner has label U:0 V:0, top right corner has label U:1 V:0, bottom left corner has label U:0 V:1, bottom right corner has label U:1 V:1.
4. "info" - 'Space - freeze cubemap' 'E/Q - changes resolution'

### Blocks
