## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grid" - a 16x16 png containing a 2x2 white/grey checkerboard pattern
2. "box" - a 16x16 png of stereotypical box, with border around the edge and X-shape in the middle
3. "uv grid" - a 256x256 png of horizontal left-to-right 0-to-255 red gradient, vertical top-to-bottom 0-to-255 green gradient, and a 4x4 grid on top of the gradient. Top left corner has label U:0 V:0, top right corner has label U:1 V:0, bottom left corner has label U:0 V:1, bottom right corner has label U:1 V:1.
4. "lines" - 4x4 image, where left and right columns of pixels are white, while the middle 2x4 pixels are empty. 2 vertical white lines

### Blocks
```scratchblocks
when flag clicked
pointerlock | set pointer lock (true v)
Simple3D | set clear color R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(0)
Simple3D | create mesh [box]
Simple3D | set [box] indices [boxIdx v]
Simple3D | set [box] positions XYZ [boxX v] [boxY v] [boxZ v]
Simple3D | set [box] texture coordinates UV [boxU v] [boxV v]
Simple3D | set [box] primitives (triangle strip v)
Simple3D | set [box] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume box)) [clamp to edge v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [box] discord pixels less opaque than [0.1], for those that pass true
Simple3D | create mesh [map]
Simple3D | set [map] from [obj mtl v] [map v]
Simple3D | set [map] texture (Simple3D | texture from costume (costume grid)) [repeat v] [pixelated v]
Simple3D | set [map] cull (back faces v)
forever
  controls :: custom
  box movement :: custom
  draw :: custom
end

define controls
change [camRotX v] by ((mouse y) * (0.5))
change [camRotY v] by ((mouse x) * (-0.5))
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by ((0) - (camRotX)) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((0) - (camRotY)) degrees
Simple3D | transform direction X:(<key (d v) pressed?> - <key (a v) pressed?>) Y:(0) Z:(<key (s v) pressed?> - <key (w v) pressed?>) from [view space v] to [world space v]
change [camX v] by (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
change [camY v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
change [camZ v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])

define setup transforms for a box
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | move X:(16) Y:(0) Z:(0)
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (([sin v] of ((box pos) * (60))) * (10)) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((box pos) * (15)) degrees
Simple3D | move X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(-32)
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (35) degrees
Simple3D | scale X:(4) Y:(4) Z:(4)

define controls (6 directions)
set [camRotX v] to (mouse y)
set [camRotY v] to ((0) - (mouse x))
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((0) - (camRotY)) degrees
Simple3D | transform direction X:(<key (d v) pressed?> - <key (a v) pressed?>) Y:(<key (space v) pressed?> - <key (join [shift] []) pressed?>) Z:(<key (s v) pressed?> - <key (w v) pressed?>) from [view space v] to [world space v]
change [camX v] by (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
change [camY v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
change [camZ v] by (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by ((0) - (camRotX)) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((0) - (camRotY)) degrees
Simple3D | move X:((0) - (camX)) Y:((0) - (camY)) Z:((0) - (camZ))

define draw
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
Simple3D | clear (color and depth v)
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.1) far: (200)
Simple3D | configure [worldToView v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by ((0) - (camRotX)) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by ((0) - (camRotY)) degrees
Simple3D | move X:((0) - (camX)) Y:((0) - (camY)) Z:((0) - (camZ))
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
setup transforms for a map :: custom
Simple3D | draw mesh [map]
setup transforms for a box :: custom
Simple3D | depth test (everything v) write (false v)
Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(0.4) G:(0.8) B:(0.4) A:(1)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(0)
Simple3D | draw mesh [box]
Simple3D | depth test (closer v) write (true v)
Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(0) G:(0) B:(0) A:(0)
Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(1) G:(1) B:(1) A:(1)
Simple3D | draw mesh [box]

define setup transforms for a map
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | scale X:(10) Y:(10) Z:(10)

define box movement
set [box pos v] to ((timer) mod (5))
if <(box pos) > [1]> then
  set [box pos v] to [1]
end
change [box pos v] by ([floor v] of ((timer) / (5)))
set [box pos v] to ((box pos) * (2))

// 3D model used:\n\nName: Simple AFPS Level\nAuthor: drummyfish\nLink: https://opengameart.org/content/simple-afps-level\nLicense: CC0

```

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grid" - a 16x16 png containing a 2x2 white/grey checkerboard pattern
2. "box" - a 16x16 png of stereotypical box, with border around the edge and X-shape in the middle
3. "uv grid" - a 256x256 png of horizontal left-to-right 0-to-255 red gradient, vertical top-to-bottom 0-to-255 green gradient, and a 4x4 grid on top of the gradient. Top left corner has label U:0 V:0, top right corner has label U:1 V:0, bottom left corner has label U:0 V:1, bottom right corner has label U:1 V:1.
4. "lines" - 4x4 image, where left and right columns of pixels are white, while the middle 2x4 pixels are empty. 2 vertical white lines

### Blocks
```scratchblocks
when flag clicked
set [higher quality v] to [1]
pointerlock | set pointer lock (true v)
```

## Sprite: "Sprite1"

### Costumes
1. "grid" - a 16x16 png containing a 2x2 white/grey checkerboard pattern
2. "box" - a 16x16 png of stereotypical box, with border around the edge and X-shape in the middle
3. "outline" - purple portal outline, empty on the inside
4. "outline2" - purple portal outline, filled with black on the inside
5. "outline3" - lime-colored portal outline, empty on the inside
6. "outline4" - lime-colored portal outline, filled with black on the inside

```scratchblocks
define start with oblique perspective FOV: (FOV) near: (NEAR) far: (FAR) plane: (A) (B) (C) (D)

if <(D) > [0]> then
  set [_A v] to ((0) - (A))
  set [_B v] to ((0) - (B))
  set [_C v] to ((0) - (C))
  set [_D v] to ((0) - (D))

  set [_A v] to (A)
  set [_B v] to (B)
  set [_C v] to (C)
  set [_D v] to (D)

}

define box movement
set [box pos v] to ((timer) mod (5))
if <(box pos) > [1]> then
  set [box pos v] to [1]
end
change [box pos v] by ([floor v] of ((timer) / (5)))
set [box pos v] to ((box pos) * (2))

define draw map

  Simple3D | scale X:(10) Y:(10) Z:(10)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [map]
}

define draw scene
draw map :: custom
draw box :: custom

when [1 v] key pressed
Simple3D | transform X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(-5) from [view space v] to [world space v]
set [p1 pos x v] to (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
set [p1 pos y v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
set [p1 pos z v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
set [p1 rot x v] to (camRotX)
set [p1 rot y v] to (camRotY)

when [2 v] key pressed
Simple3D | transform X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(-5) from [view space v] to [world space v]
set [p2 pos x v] to (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
set [p2 pos y v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
set [p2 pos z v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
set [p2 rot x v] to ((0) - (camRotX))
set [p2 rot y v] to ((camRotY) + (180))

define set clipping plane
Simple3D | transform X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(0) from [model space v] to [view space v]
set [_X v] to (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
set [_Y v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
set [_Z v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
Simple3D | transform direction X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(1) from [model space v] to [view space v]
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
start with oblique perspective FOV: [90] near: [0.1] far: [200] plane: (Simple3D | transformed [X v]) (Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) (Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) ((0) - (((Simple3D | transformed [X v]) * (_X)) + (((Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) * (_Y)) + ((Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) * (_Z))))) :: custom
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation

define reset transforms

replace item (15) of [_matrix v] with [0]
Simple3D | start with saved in [_matrix v] at (1)
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation

define test reset
Simple3D | configure [viewToProjected v] transformation
Simple3D | start with perspective FOV: (90) near: (0.1) far: (200)
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation

define check plane
Simple3D | configure [modelToWorld v] transformation
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | move X:(p1 pos x) Y:(p1 pos y) Z:(p1 pos z)
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (p1 rot y) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (p1 rot x) degrees
Simple3D | transform X:(camX) Y:(camY) Z:(camZ) from [world space v] to [model space v]
set [_X v] to (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
set [_Y v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
set [_Z v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
Simple3D | transform direction X:(0) Y:(0) Z:(1) from [view space v] to [model space v]
set [_X2 v] to (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
set [_Y2 v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
set [_Z2 v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
if <<([abs v] of (_X)) < [5]> and <([abs v] of (_Y)) < [5]>> then
  if <((p1 side) * (_Z)) < [0]> then
    Simple3D | start with no transformation
    Simple3D | move X:(p2 pos x) Y:(p2 pos y) Z:(p2 pos z)
    Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (p2 rot y) degrees
    Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (p2 rot x) degrees
    Simple3D | transform X:(_X) Y:(_Y) Z:(_Z) from [model space v] to [world space v]
    set [camX v] to (Simple3D | transformed [X v])
    set [camY v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
    set [camZ v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])
    Simple3D | transform direction X:(_X2) Y:(_Y2) Z:(_Z2) from [model space v] to [world space v]
    set [camRotY v] to (([atan v] of ((Simple3D | transformed [X v]) / (Simple3D | transformed [Z v]))) + (<(Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) < [0]> * (180)))
    set [_dist v] to ([sqrt v] of (((Simple3D | transformed [X v]) * (Simple3D | transformed [X v])) + ((Simple3D | transformed [Z v]) * (Simple3D | transformed [Z v]))))
    set [camRotX v] to ((0) - (([atan v] of ((Simple3D | transformed [Y v]) / (_dist))) + (<(_dist) < [0]> * (180))))
    set [p1 side v] to [0]
  else
    set [p1 side v] to (_Z)
  end
else
  set [p1 side v] to [0]
end
Simple3D | start with no transformation
Simple3D | move X:(p2 pos x) Y:(p2 pos y) Z:(p2 pos z)
Simple3D | rotate around [Y v] by (p2 rot y) degrees
Simple3D | rotate around [X v] by (p2 rot x) degrees

set [_Y v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])
set [_Z v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Z v])

set [_Y2 v] to (Simple3D | transformed [Y v])

    Simple3D | start with no transformation

define draw plane (x) (y) (z) (rx) (ry) (mesh)
```

```scratchblocks
define init pixel states
delete all of [init colors v]
repeat (x res)
  repeat (y res)
    if <(pick random (1) to (2)) = [1]> then
      add [255] to [init colors v]
      add [255] to [init colors v]
      add [255] to [init colors v]
      add [255] to [init colors v]
    else
      add [0] to [init colors v]
      add [0] to [init colors v]
      add [0] to [init colors v]
      add [255] to [init colors v]
    end
  end
end

  count neighbors :: custom

define generate new main
lmscomments | comment [upper bound] {
  Simple3D | render to texture of (output main)
  Simple3D | clear (color v)
  Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(((3.5) / (8)) * (256)) G:(((3.5) / (8)) * (256)) B:(((3.5) / (8)) * (256)) A:(0)
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(-256) G:(-256) B:(-256) A:(1)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [counter]
}
lmscomments | comment [distort lower bound] {
  Simple3D | render to texture of [counter]
  Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:((1) / (8)) G:((1) / (8)) B:((1) / (8)) A:(0)
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:((-1) / (8)) G:((-1) / (8)) B:((-1) / (8)) A:(1)
  Simple3D | set (input main) blending (subtractive v)
  Simple3D | draw mesh (input main)
  Simple3D | set (input main) blending (default v)
}
lmscomments | comment [lower bound] {
  Simple3D | render to texture of (output main)
  Simple3D | set global color [adder v] R:(((-1.5) / (8)) * (256)) G:(((-1.5) / (8)) * (256)) B:(((-1.5) / (8)) * (256)) A:(0)
  Simple3D | set global color [multiplier v] R:(256) G:(256) B:(256) A:(1)
  Simple3D | draw mesh [counter multiply]
}

define render to stage

Simple3D | draw mesh (output main)
Simple3D | start with no transformation

set [x res v] to [120]
set [y res v] to [90]
set pixel [3] [3] :: custom
set pixel [4] [3] :: custom

define set pixel (x) (y)
replace item ((((x) + ((x res) * (y))) * (4)) + (1)) of [init colors v] with [255]
replace item ((((x) + ((x res) * (y))) * (4)) + (2)) of [init colors v] with [255]
replace item ((((x) + ((x res) * (y))) * (4)) + (3)) of [init colors v] with [255]

when [up arrow v] key pressed

  set [zoom v] to ((zoom) * (2))
end

when [down arrow v] key pressed
if <not <key (down arrow v) pressed?>> then
  set [zoom v] to ((zoom) / (2))
end

define controls
if <mouse down?> then
  set [tmp v] to (((runtimeoptions | stage (height v)) / (2)) * (zoom))
  change [scroll x v] by (((mouse x) - (mouse x :: variables)) / (tmp))
  change [scroll y v] by (((mouse y) - (mouse y :: variables)) / (tmp))
end
set [mouse x v] to (mouse x)
set [mouse y v] to (mouse y)
```
